  > DECISION 2026-09-19 (k) — Per-lane guardrails, relative revert, seat-idle monitoring, disclosure of proof-latency-bound search
  > (Scheduling and reporting only. Record verbatim in docs/DECISIONS.md.)
  >
  > 1. Guardrails of (j) become per lane: a lane pauses admission when its own unverified-at-build fraction (last hour) exceeds 35 % or its proof-queue estimate exceeds 60 minutes, and resumes when both clear; no tier-wide pause. The cap of 24 generating runs is tier-wide.
  > 2. Revert rule of (j)-1d becomes relative: revert to counting waiting runs only if some arm-model row's unverified-at-build fraction is more than 20 points worse than that row's baseline over 05:49–02:09 for three consecutive hours. A row at 100 % that was at 100 % before (j) is not a revert trigger.
  > 3. Add to the hourly slots report and §7c: idle seat-minutes per lane per hour, and the unverified-at-build fraction per design. Designs whose median proof latency exceeds the 1800 s generation window are marked "proof-latency-bound search" in §0a with the wording: "on this design the archive stayed empty during generation for all arms; parents were D; the search reduces to E4-guided one-shot rewriting".
  > 4. No change to the generation cadence during Phase 5. Record for Phase 6: an ablation on the proof-latency-bound designs with a verdict-synchronous cadence (generate only when ≥ 60 % of the previous generation has verdicts, cap 4 h), to measure informed versus blind search; and the map_by_name-first proof for classes a and b, targeted at the class-b inconclusive concentration (SPI 99 %, simple_spi 84 % under v2).
  > 5. ETA stands at Stage B final ≈ 09-20 11:40; report at the next rebalance whether idle seat-minutes appeared in any lane while runs were queued.

The pool was restarted 05:07 with the new order (16 slots at once: more than 12 DC seats idle by the seat count; pool E4s run at priority 1 below the runs' own E4s, so their throughput is what the DC seats leave). Hourly, the slot report and §7c carry the pool's throughput and the medium-tier B0 E4 ETA against the Stage B proof ETA (`pool_progress`, `stage_b_proof_eta`: remaining proof seat-hours / current shares, latest lane). At 05:08: E4 records in the last hour — medium B0 34, large B0 8, other pool groups 31; last 3 h medium B0 116 (38.7 / h); medium B0 E4 waiting 1 065 plus about 316 expected from the 9 medium B0 runs not yet done → ETA 2026-09-20 16:52 (35.7 h) against the Stage B proof ETA 2026-09-20 12:03 (30.9 h): **the B0 E4 ETA is later — Stage B final waits for it (D3)**; the medium rate should rise now that medium B0 goes first with 16 slots, and the line is re-evaluated every hour. *Item 2.* The 11 candidates (arm_cpu2 7, eth_cop 4; label nonequiv, no verdict, sim job crashed 10:41) carry the note "[resim pending (DECISION 2026-09-19 (m) 2) …]" and form the pool group `resim`, first in the order: the failed job's payload is re-submitted as a sim job at the run pipeline's priority (4, not niced); a passing simulation clears the nonequiv label and submits E4 (the run's own fitness record, payload `resim`, priority 4) and the SEQ proof (the sim record attached as the run does, priority 4 + the undiagnosed offset, the candidate's own seq cap) in parallel into the normal VC Formal queue (their lane: the window); outcomes are written into the rows (V1 / V2, verdict, V3 fields, harness_version 2, notes); a failing simulation writes the verdict and keeps nonequiv; a proven candidate keeps no arm label (uniform rule A labels it in the reports). At the restart 16 E4 jobs of the previous order were in flight, so the first passes submitted nothing; the re-simulations go as slots free (reported below in STATUS). *Item 3.* Cause of the gap (40 candidates at 05:00; 2 of the 42 received their E4 from the large-tier retry group meanwhile): every one of them had exactly one E4 attempt by its run — aes 32 timed out (dc_shell exceeded the 1 020 s guard; aes E4 averages 13 min and exceeds the guard under load), the other 8 failed with exit 1 (eval_failed) — the driver's single retry (DECISION 2026-09-18 (b) item 4) fires only on a timeout and only while the run's driver is alive (the aes results arrived after the runs had finished), and the pool's retry group covered only the large tier (its `tier` setting) and sat last in the pool's order, so the medium ones (eth_cop 3, sub_32bit 2, mc_adr_sel 1, UART 1, thresholds 1) had no retry lane at all. Fix: the retry group is always in scope, every tier, right after the medium B0 E4 — `e4_timeout` for a proven candidate of a finished run whose E4 was attempted (an evaluation record of any status or a DC job: re-run with the 3 600 s guard, e4_rerun and force_rerun), `e4_late` for one whose E4 was never submitted (normal E4); B0 candidates are in b0_e4 whatever their attempts.
